- **textarea**: Use the textarea() factory for multi-line plain text fields like descriptions or bios. Use when defining Storyblok schema fields. ([jimdrury/jimdrury-component-schema](https://github.com/jimdrury/jimdrury-component-schema/tree/HEAD/skills/textarea))
- **textbook-auditor**: The textbook conventions and style auditor (read-only). Checks, clause by clause, whether a written or revised textbook keeps the dual-audience convention (self-standing prose, isolated fundamentals) and the style rules (prose, no translationese, assertion titles, established imagery versus decorative metaphor), whether any term appears for the first time without explanation, and whether the figures and the edition labelling are correct. Call it before issuing a new edition or after writing a whole textbook. It audits and reports only — it does not modify the textbook. ([H-Hur/study-kit](https://github.com/H-Hur/study-kit/tree/HEAD/plugins/study-kit-codex/skills/textbook-auditor) · ★4)
- **textbook-authoring**: The procedure for writing a dual-audience textbook (HTML) from the learner profile and the curriculum, and managing its editions. Use it for requests like "make me a textbook", "write the preview material", or "write the next chapter". It covers the dual-audience technique of isolating fundamentals into groundwork boxes, the standard skeleton of a chapter, the prose style rules, designing interactive figures so they become still frames in print, edition labelling, and the finished textbook template. ([H-Hur/study-kit](https://github.com/H-Hur/study-kit/tree/HEAD/plugins/study-kit/skills/textbook-authoring) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **textbook-chapter-writer**: Drafts an undergraduate textbook chapter -- learning objectives, motivation, worked examples, exercises -- for a student who is studying the topic, not yet doing it. Diataxis-wise this is explanation with worked application, not a tutorial; if the user wants a hands-on lesson the reader follows at a keyboard, use `tutorial-writer` instead. May cite grounding papers from the synced corpus (content/ledger.sqlite via chitragupta.retrieval.search()) for motivation/background, but is not citation-dense; most content is original worked examples and exercises. Triggers when the user asks to draft a… ([prasadtalasila/chitragupta](https://github.com/prasadtalasila/chitragupta/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/textbook-chapter-writer) · ★1)
- **textbook-craft**: How the best math/CS textbooks (Halmos, Axler, SICP, Knuth, Feynman, Sipser, CLRS, MacKay, Pólya, Lakatos, Hersh) open a chapter, place worked examples and exercises, and state honest boundaries — applied to the Harbor Book's chapters. Use when drafting or reviewing a chapter opener, deciding where an exercise or worked example belongs, auditing a chapter's page grammar or claim-labeling, or running scripts/chapter_lint.py against a .tex chapter. NOT for the seven-moves result-writeup style (use harbor-exposition), figure drawing (use harbor-chartwork), or LaTeX build mechanics (use latex-whi… ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/tree/HEAD/skills/textbook-craft) · ★2)
- **textbook-evidence-hierarchy**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/textbook-evidence-hierarchy) · ★729)
- **textbook-methodology**: Use when drafting prose sections for a bookwright (technical non-fiction) project. Covers atom-outward design, deferral discipline, running threads, page budgets, per-section subagent cadence, and commit discipline. ([queelius/claude-anvil](https://github.com/queelius/claude-anvil/tree/HEAD/bookwright/skills/textbook-methodology) · ★2)
- **textbook-notes**: Use when the user asks to summarize/distill a textbook chapter or subchapter (e.g. "ch4", "4.2") into a study note, asks follow-up questions to revise a note, appends chapters, or rebuilds note HTML. Distills dense, math-heavy textbook chapters (PDF) into Notion-style study notes with callouts, KaTeX math, gap-filled derivations, and Q&A blocks. Markdown source, self-contained offline HTML output, printable to PDF. Korean triggers - 전공서적 정리, 교과서 정리, 챕터 요약, 정리 노트, N장 정리, 서브챕터 정리, 이해 안 되는 부분 질문. ([Cotidie/textbook-to-note-skill](https://github.com/Cotidie/textbook-to-note-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/textbook-notes) · 3 copies)
- **textbook-publish**: The procedure for turning the textbook HTML into two PDF editions, exercise and answer, and sending them to the learner's channel. Use it for requests like "send me the textbook", "make it a PDF", or "ship the revised edition". It covers the two-edition principle that exploits collapsed answers, the headless Chrome conversion commands, page-count verification, and four measured traps including the one where collapsed content vanishes in print. ([H-Hur/study-kit](https://github.com/H-Hur/study-kit/tree/HEAD/plugins/study-kit/skills/textbook-publish) · ★4 · 2 copies)
